1042:     private record ExitDecision(boolean exit,String reason,String explanation,BigDecimal newTakeProfit){
1043:         ExitDecision(boolean exit,String reason,String explanation){this(exit,reason,explanation,null);}
1044:         static ExitDecision hold(){return new ExitDecision(false,"HOLD","Position remains open.",null);}
1045:     }
1046:     private record ShadowPosition(long positionId, Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity,
1047:                                   BigDecimal cost, int positionPercent, BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit,
1048:                                   BigDecimal highest, boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
1049:                                   ProfitLockState profitLockState, Instant profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1050:                                   int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
1051:                                   int entryMomentum, int entryVolume, NearTpState nearTpState, BigDecimal nearTpBestPrice,
1052:                                   int nearTpBearishStreak, Long nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, boolean nearTpHarvestUsed,
1053:                                   BigDecimal nearTpHarvestedQuantity, BigDecimal partialRealizedPnl,
1054:                                   BigDecimal partialHarvestCostBasis) {
1055:         ShadowPosition withLock(BigDecimal h, boolean a, BigDecimal l, ProfitLockState state, Instant rebaseStartedAt) {
1056:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit,
1057:                     h, a, l, state, rebaseStartedAt,
1058:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1059:                     nearTpState, nearTpBestPrice, nearTpBearishStreak, nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, nearTpHarvestUsed,
1060:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1061:         }
1062:         ShadowPosition withTakeProfit(BigDecimal tp) {
1063:             // FIX-117 Replay parity: TP extension preserves already-earned Near-TP protection
1064:             // exactly like Production. REJECTION_DETECTED keeps its state/best price but clears
1065:             // bearish persistence accumulated under the old geometry. INACTIVE and
1066:             // FAILURE_CONFIRMED keep the pre-FIX-117 reset behavior. Harvested state is terminal.
1067:             NearTpState state = nearTpState;
1068:             BigDecimal bestPrice = nearTpBestPrice;
1069:             int bearishStreak = nearTpBearishStreak;
1070:             Long lastOneMinuteSignalId = nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId;
1071: 
1072:             if (!nearTpHarvestUsed) {
1073:                 switch (nearTpState) {
1074:                     case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
1075:                         state = NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1076:                         bestPrice = null;
1077:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1078:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1079:                     }
1080:                     case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> { }
1081:                     case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
1082:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1083:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1084:                     }
1085:                     case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> { }
1086:                 }
1087:             }
1088: 
1089:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, tp,
1090:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1091:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1092:                     state, bestPrice, bearishStreak, lastOneMinuteSignalId, nearTpHarvestUsed,
1093:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1094:         }
1095:         ShadowPosition withAdd(BigDecimal newEntry, BigDecimal newQuantity, BigDecimal newCost, int newPercent, BigDecimal newStop, BigDecimal newTakeProfit) {
1096:             NearTpState state = nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpState : NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1097:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
1098:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1099:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1100:                     state, nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpBestPrice : null, nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpBearishStreak : 0,
1101:                     nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId : null, nearTpHarvestUsed, nearTpHarvestedQuantity,
1102:                     partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1103:         }
1104:         ShadowPosition withNearTp(NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state) {
1105:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit,
1106:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1107:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1108:                     state.nearTpState(), state.bestPrice(), state.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(), state.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId(),
1109:                     state.harvestUsed(), nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1110:         }
1111:         ShadowPosition withPartialHarvest(BigDecimal soldQty, BigDecimal soldCost, BigDecimal realizedPnl) {
1112:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity.subtract(soldQty, MC), cost.subtract(soldCost, MC),
1113:                     positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit, highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
1114:                     profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1115:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1116:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED, nearTpBestPrice, nearTpBearishStreak, nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, true,
1117:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity.add(soldQty, MC), partialRealizedPnl.add(realizedPnl, MC),
1118:                     partialHarvestCostBasis.add(soldCost, MC));
1119:         }
1120:         boolean sameNearTp(ShadowPosition other) {
1121:             return nearTpState == other.nearTpState
1122:                     && equalsDecimal(nearTpBestPrice, other.nearTpBestPrice)
1123:                     && nearTpBearishStreak == other.nearTpBearishStreak
1124:                     && java.util.Objects.equals(nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, other.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId)
1125:                     && nearTpHarvestUsed == other.nearTpHarvestUsed;
1126:         }
1127:         private boolean equalsDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
1128:             return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
1129:         }
1130:     }
1131: 
1132: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java =====

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.market.service;
   2: 
   3: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   4: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   8: import java.sql.Timestamp;
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Locale;
  12: 
  13: /**
  14:  * FIX-052 exact Replay/Production parity support.
  15:  *
  16:  * Production protects open positions from every canonical 1m Binance live-price
  17:  * update, not only from candle-close analysis signals. Persisting that exact feed
  18:  * lets Replay evaluate TP/SL/profit-lock against the same observations and order.
  19:  * Timestamps are always stored/interpreted as UTC Instants; timezone conversion is
  20:  * a presentation concern only.
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @org.springframework.context.annotation.DependsOn("localTraderInstance")
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: public class MarketPriceEventService {
  26:     private static final int PRICE_SCALE=12;
  27:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  28:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  29: 
  30:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode="OFF";
  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-max-age-seconds:15}") private long maxAge=15;
  32:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-future-skew-seconds:2}") private long futureSkew=2;
  33:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(MarketPriceEventService.class);
  34:     private final java.util.Set<Long> inFlight=java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  35:     public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
  36:     public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
  37:         return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  38:     }
  39:     private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
  40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
  41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
  42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
  43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
  44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
  45:         return null;
  46:     }
  47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
  48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
  49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
  50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
  51:         BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
  52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
  53:         if(invalid!=null){
  54:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
  56:             log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
  57:             return null;
  58:         }
  59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
  60:         try {
  61:             jdbcTemplate.update(connection->{
  62:                 var ps=connection.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PENDING',1)",new String[]{"id"});
  63:                 ps.setString(1,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(2,Timestamp.from(observed));ps.setBigDecimal(3,normalizedPrice);ps.setString(4,DIRECT_SOURCE);return ps;
  64:             },keys);
  65:             long id=java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(keys.getKey(),"generated price id").longValue();inFlight.add(id);return id;
  66:         }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){
  67:             var first=jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,price FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND ordering_slot=1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed));
  68:             if(first.size()!=1)throw duplicate;
  69:             BigDecimal existing=(BigDecimal)first.getFirst().get("price");
  70:             log.warn("[FIX-153][EQUAL_TIMESTAMP_REJECTED] symbol={}, observedUtc={}, firstEvent={}, firstPrice={}, rejectedPrice={}, observationAgeMs={}, priceDisagreement={}",symbol,observed,first.getFirst().get("id"),existing,normalizedPrice,java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis(),existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)!=0);
  71:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot,outcome_reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'NON_CANONICAL',NULL,?)",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),normalizedPrice,DIRECT_SOURCE,existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)==0?"DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP":"AMBIGUOUS_EQUAL_TIMESTAMP");
  72:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);return null;
  73:         }
  74:     }
  75:     public void release(long id){inFlight.remove(id);}
  76:     /** Runs after existing symbol/position lock at READ_COMMITTED, never counts unresolved receipts. */
  77:     public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){
  78:         return !jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at>? AND source IN ('BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE',?) AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') ORDER BY observed_at DESC LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),DIRECT_SOURCE).isEmpty();
  79:     }
  80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
  81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
  82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
  83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
  84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
  86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
  87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
  88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
  89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
  90:             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){
  91:                 if(status==STATUS_COMMITTED){
  92:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count("APPLIED".equals(state)?com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_APPLIED:com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  93:                     if("APPLIED".equals(state))com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.protection(symbol,observed);
  94:                     log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, observedKsa={}, outcome={}, reason={}",id,symbol,observed,observed.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),state,reason);

 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));

 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();

 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.

 611:

 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

 617:                 } finally {

 619:                 }

 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }

 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""

 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
 652:                             generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),

 654:                             generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),

 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while

 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never

 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

 233:                 // Production's candle-close PaperTradingService has one additional SELL authority
 234:                 // after live-price protection: validate the newly generated 1m bearish signal.
 235:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 236:                 if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 237:                     ExitDecision exit = new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 238:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 239:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 240:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 241:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 242:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 243:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 244:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 245:                     trades++;
 246:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 247:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
 248:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 249:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
 250:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
 251:                                         open = null;
 252:                     // Same production invocation cannot both SELL and immediately BUY again.
 253:                     continue;
 254:                 }
 255:             }
 256: 
 257:             // FIX-014 replay parity: production may use a fresh 5m BUY transition only to
 258:             // wake an already-live deferred 1m opportunity. The actual execution/risk plan
 259:             // remains the latest fresh 1m signal returned by the shared production service.
 260:             // All other non-1m signals remain context-only exactly as before.
 261:             TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
 262:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision decision;
 263:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 264:                 int currentAllocation = open == null ? 0 : open.positionPercent();
 265:                 String currentStage = replayStage(currentAllocation);
 266:                 // FIX-026 parity: Recovery/Transition Entry is intentionally NOT reimplemented here.
 267:                 // Replay enters the exact production ExecutionIntelligenceService, which delegates to
 268:                 // RecoveryTransitionService using only candles closed as-of this historical signal.
 269:                 // This protects the ENA 12:55-13:04 KSA regression from replay-only future leakage.
 270:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal, currentAllocation, currentStage);
 271:             } else if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && open == null) {
 272:                 ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup =
 273:                         executionIntelligenceService.evaluateSetupTimeframeWakeup(signal, 0);
 274:                 if (!wakeup.present()) {
 275:                     // FIX-023 replay parity: use the exact same production confirmation wake-up
 276:                     // after liquidity/HTF improves; 5m remains context-only execution authority.
 277:                     wakeup = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(signal, 0);
 278:                 }
 279:                 if (!wakeup.present()) continue;
 280:                 executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
 281:                 decision = wakeup.decision();
 282:             } else {
 283:                 continue;
 284:             }
 285: 
 286:             // Warm-up evaluates the SAME production decision service so evidence/opportunity
 287:             // memory is realistic at executionStart, but it never opens or adds to a wallet
 288:             // position before the requested test window. The trigger timestamp (signal) controls
 289:             // the replay window; executionSignal owns price/risk just like production.
 290:             boolean inExecutionWindow = !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
 291:                     && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd);
 292:             LocalDate signalTradeDate = signal.getGeneratedAt().atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
 293:             if (!signalTradeDate.equals(replayTradeDate)) {
 294:                 replayTradeDate = signalTradeDate;
 295:                 replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
 296:                 replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
 297:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
 298:                         replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
 299:             }
 300:             // IMPORTANT: Proven/Regression never re-implements pressure readiness, normal BUY
 301:             // routing, or FIX-014 wake-up rules. All are executed by the production
 302:             // ExecutionIntelligenceService above; only wallet/opportunity persistence is shadowed.
 303:             if (!inExecutionWindow) continue;
 304: 
 305:             if (open == null && decision.allowed()) {
 306:                 // FIX-056 Replay parity: resolve the same canonical execution-price authority
 307:                 // after the same Production decision gates, then revalidate Entry Quality at
 308:                 // that price before sizing. A replay without a fresh price event cannot claim
 309:                 // exact execution parity and therefore does not open the position here.
 310:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 311:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 312:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 313:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(executionSignal, decision, fresh.price());
 314:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 315:                 int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0 ? 100 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
 316:                 int effectivePercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100,
 317:                         (int)Math.round(atrPercent * decision.positionPercent() / 100.0)));
 318:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 319:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 320:                         effectivePercent, 0, true, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 321:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 322:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 323:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 324:                     BigDecimal qty = sizing.quantity();
 325:                     effectivePercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 326:                     long positionId = openPosition(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent);
 327:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent, decision);
 328:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, decision);
 329:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 330:                     replayExecutedNewPositions++;
 331:                     // Keep the complete immutable BUY thesis needed by production continuation.
 332:                     // Replay must not drop entry structure and silently evaluate a different exit policy.
 333:                     open = new ShadowPosition(positionId, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), fresh.price(), qty, budget,
 334:                             effectivePercent, executionSignal.getStopLoss(), executionSignal.getTakeProfit(), fresh.price(), false, null,
 335:                             ProfitLockState.INACTIVE, null,
 336:                             executionSignal.getTotalScore(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), executionSignal.getTrendScore(), executionSignal.getTrendStructureScore(),
 337:                             executionSignal.getMomentumScore(), executionSignal.getVolumeScore(),
 338:                             NearTpState.INACTIVE, null, 0, null, false, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 339:                     // FIX-112A: only an actually opened shadow position consumes the bullish entry.
 340:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(executionSignal.getId());
 341:                 }
 342:             } else if (open != null && decision.allowed()) {
 343:                 // FIX-056: progressive Replay adds use the same fresh execution price and
 344:                 // execution-time Entry Quality revalidation as Production.
 345:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 346:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 347:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 348:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, decision, fresh.price());
 349:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 350:                 int addPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100 - open.positionPercent(), decision.positionPercent()));
 351:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 352:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 353:                         addPercent, open.positionPercent(), false, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 354:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 355:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 356:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 357:                     addPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 358:                     BigDecimal addedQty = sizing.quantity();
 359:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, signal, fresh, addedQty, budget, addPercent, decision);
 360:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, decision);
 361:                     // FIX-112A: a progressive add consumes only its own triggering signal.
 362:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(signal.getId());
 363:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 364:                     open = addToPosition(runId, open, signal, addedQty, budget, addPercent);
 365:                 }
 366:             }
 367:         }
 368:         }
 369: 
 370:         BigDecimal finalWallet = cash;
 371:         if (open != null && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 372:             BigDecimal mark = timeline.get(timeline.size() - 1).getLatestPrice();
 373:             if (mark != null) finalWallet = finalWallet.add(mark.multiply(open.quantity(), MC), MC);
 374:         }

   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;

  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}

 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;

 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }

 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }
 387: 
 388:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 389:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }

 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:1-110 =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:1-100 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   8: import javax.sql.DataSource;
   9: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-122: bounded, non-locking committed ledger reads; no managed-position row locks.
  15:  * STOP_LOSS is the terminal reason written by executeSignalLinkedExit. The distinct
  16:  * POSITION_STOP_LOSS and partial-harvest reasons are deliberately not included.
  17:  */
  18: @Service
  19: public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
  20:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  21:     private final TransactionTemplate read;
  22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
  23: 
  24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
  26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
  31:         read.setTimeout(2);
  32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  34:         write.setTimeout(2);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
  45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
  58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
  62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
  63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
  64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
  65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
  66:     }
  67: }

codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-11-- The most recent 210-candle data-quality window has a synchronized gap for every enabled symbol: missing 1m open `2026-10-09T04:34:00Z` (KSA 07:34) and missing 5m open `2026-10-09T04:30:00Z` (KSA 07:30).
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:12:- Cause confirmed from the WebSocket lifecycle log: generation 1 was revoked at `2026-10-09T04:35:00.133951100Z`; generation 2 was admitted at `04:35:01.369103400Z` (1,236 ms interruption). The affected 1m and 5m candles had both closed at the reconnect boundary. Binance did not replay their final closed-kline events, leaving the already-created local rows open rather than finalized.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-13-- `CandleDataQualityService` is configured with `minimum-candles: 210` and `maximum-missing-candles: 0`. Therefore one missing candle makes the entire relevant analysis window invalid. Runtime logs confirm `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED` with `Missing 1 candle(s) in the analysis window` for current 1m and 5m worker attempts.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-14-- 1h has no comparable gap and produced a live signal at `2026-10-09T06:00:03Z` (KSA 09:00).
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-15-- No repair or configuration change was made. Correct remediation must restore authoritative missing candles rather than relaxing the zero-missing data-quality safety rule.

codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-23-# ETHUSDT paper-trade 59 investigation — 2026-10-09
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-24-
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:25:- Scope: local `crypto_ai` paper trade `wallet_trade.id=59` for `ETHUSDT`; all database reads were bounded and read-only. Stored UTC timestamps are shown below with their KSA (`UTC+03:00`) interpretation.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-26-- Entry: signal `trade_signal.id=69226` was generated at `2026-10-09T07:33:00.233691Z` (10:33:00.234 KSA) from the 1m candle opened at 10:32 KSA. It was a `BUY`, score 79, confidence 70, entry allowed, at 2502.77. The trade executed at `07:33:01.654548Z` (10:33:01.655 KSA), 1.42 seconds after the signal; this is not an execution-latency issue.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-27-- Exit: `wallet_trade.id=60` closed the position at 2504.30 at `07:34:34.196100Z` (10:34:34.196 KSA) with `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. The managed position recorded a high of 2505.80 and an activated profit-lock price of 2504.429502243252. The realized paper gain was about 0.02445 USDT before fees (0.015982291620 ETH x 1.53 USDT).
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-28-- Market path: ETH rose from 2485.88 at 07:54 KSA to 2502.77 at entry (+16.89, +0.68%). It reached 2505.56 at 10:25 KSA before the entry; entry was 2.79 (0.11%) below that high. The post-entry high was 2505.93 at 10:34 KSA, only 3.16 (0.13%) above entry, then the candle retraced. This supports the user's observation that entry occurred late in the observed move.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-29-- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-30-- Immediate signal sequence after recovery: at 10:30 KSA the 5m signal was a BUY but blocked by `RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION`; at 10:31 KSA the 1m raw BUY was vetoed by a bearish BTC context; at 10:33 KSA BTC context became neutral and the 1m trend-following BUY was allowed. The entry signal itself explicitly recorded mixed higher-timeframe context (5m BUY, 1h NEUTRAL) and a weakening but ultra-close ask wall before its target.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:31:- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54–10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.

codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-189-  whereas live Production samples its in-memory/live Binance source. Such runs
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:190:  are useful simulations but are not full signal-parity proof unless the
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md-191-  required historical evidence exists and is reported.
codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:192:- Confirmed price-evidence limitation: a Replay run labels itself

1021:     private boolean hasUsableSentimentCoverage(boolean sentimentEnabled, SentimentOverview overview) {
1022:         if (!sentimentEnabled || overview == null || overview.providers() == null) return false;
1023:         var enabled = overview.providers().stream().filter(provider -> provider.enabled()).toList();

1025: 
1026:         var usable = enabled.stream()
1027:                 .filter(provider -> provider.sampleCount() > 0)
1028:                 .filter(provider -> provider.confidence() != null
1029:                         && provider.confidence().compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.40)) >= 0)
1030:                 .filter(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight() != null
1031:                         && provider.effectiveWeight().compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.05)) >= 0)
1032:                 .toList();
1033:         if (usable.isEmpty()) return false;
1034: 
1035:         double coverage = usable.size() / (double) enabled.size();
1036:         BigDecimal totalEffectiveWeight = usable.stream()
1037:                 .map(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight())
1038:                 .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
1039:         return coverage >= 0.50 && totalEffectiveWeight.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.05)) >= 0;
1040:     }

 178:         }

 202:                 denominator = denominator.add(effectiveWeight, MC);
 203:             }

 206:                     providerName,

 223:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 224:                 : total.divide(denominator, MC);
 225:         return new Aggregation(score, signals.size(), breakdown);
 226:     }

 107:     public ProviderHealth health(SentimentProviderConfig config, Instant now) {
 108:         if (!properties.enabled() || !config.isEnabled()) {
 109:             return new ProviderHealth("DISABLED", false, 0, "Provider is disabled");
 110:         }
 111:         Instant success = config.getLastSuccessAt();
 112:         if (success == null) {
 113:             String state = "NEVER_RUN".equals(config.getLastStatus()) ? "STALE" : "DOWN";
 114:             return new ProviderHealth(state, false, -1,
 115:                     "No successful collection has been recorded");
 116:         }
 117:         java.time.Duration age = java.time.Duration.between(success, now);
 118:         long hours = Math.max(0, age.toHours());
 119:         if (age.compareTo(properties.health().downAfter()) >= 0) {
 120:             return new ProviderHealth("DOWN", false, hours,
 121:                     "No successful collection for " + hours + " hours");
 122:         }
 123:         if (age.compareTo(properties.health().staleAfter()) >= 0) {
 124:             return new ProviderHealth("STALE", false, hours,
 125:                     "Latest successful data is " + hours + " hours old");
 126:         }
 127:         if ("FAILED".equals(config.getLastStatus())) {
 128:             return new ProviderHealth("DEGRADED", true, hours,
 129:                     "Latest attempt failed; recent successful data is still active");
 130:         }
 131:         return new ProviderHealth("HEALTHY", true, hours, "Provider is collecting normally");
 132:     }
 133: 
 134:     public boolean contributes(SentimentProviderConfig config, Instant now) {
 135:         return health(config, now).contributing();
 136:     }

  17: /**
  18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
  19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
  20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
  21:  */
  22: @Slf4j
  23: @Component
  24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  26:     private final Executor executor;
  27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
  28:     @Autowired(required = false)
  29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  30: 
  31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
  32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
  33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  34:         this.executor = executor;
  35:         this.metrics = metrics;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
  42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
  43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
  44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
  45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  46:         try {
  47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
  48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
  49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  51:                     path, reference.getId());
  52:         }
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
  56:         try {
  57:             jdbc.update("""
  58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
  65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  67:                     """,
  68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
  69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
  70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
  71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
  72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
  73:         } catch (Exception e) {
  74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
  77:         }
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
  81: 
  82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
  83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
  85:             Instant close = deriveCandleClose(signal);
  86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
  87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
  88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
  89:         }
  90:     }
  91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
  92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
  93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
  94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
  96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
  98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
  99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
 100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),
 101:                     assessment.effectiveOneHour().name(), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute().name(), assessment.band().name(),
 102:                     assessment.hardConfirmationPresent(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(), legacy, finalResult, trim(reason));
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105:     private static Instant deriveCandleClose(TradeSignal signal) {
 106:         if (signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null || signal.getInterval() == null) return null;
 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
 110:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
 111: }

   7: /**
   8:  * Pure FIX-154 BUY-context assessment. It deliberately returns effective
   9:  * decisions separately and never mutates the JPA-managed context signals.
  10:  */
  11: @Service
  12: public class HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService {
  13:     public static final int HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 70;
  14: 
  15:     public Assessment assess(TradeSignal fiveMinute, boolean fiveFresh,
  16:                              TradeSignal oneHour, boolean oneFresh) {
  17:         if (!fiveFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  18:         if (!oneFresh) return Assessment.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT", Band.NOT_APPLICABLE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  19: 
  20:         SignalDecision five = decision(fiveMinute);
  21:         SignalDecision one = decision(oneHour);
  22:         if (bearish(five)) {
  23:             boolean hard = hard(oneHour);
  24:             return Assessment.reject("5M_BEARISH_VETO", hard ? Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE : band(oneHour), fiveMinute, oneHour)
  25:                     .withHardConfirmation(hard);
  26:         }
  27:         if (bearish(one) && !qualityValid(oneHour)) {
  28:             return Assessment.reject("1H_BEARISH_VETO", Band.NOT_TRUSTED, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  29:         }
  30:         if (hard(oneHour)) {
  31:             return Assessment.reject("HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO", Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  32:         }
  33:         if (soft(oneHour)) {
  34:             return Assessment.allow(Band.SOFT_CONFLICT, five, SignalDecision.NEUTRAL, 25, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  35:         }
  36:         return Assessment.allow(qualityValid(oneHour) ? Band.NO_CONFLICT : Band.NOT_TRUSTED,
  37:                 five, one, null, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  38:     }
  39: 
  40:     private Band band(TradeSignal one) {
  41:         if (!qualityValid(one)) return Band.NOT_TRUSTED;
  42:         if (hard(one)) return Band.HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE;
  43:         if (soft(one)) return Band.SOFT_CONFLICT;
  44:         return Band.NO_CONFLICT;
  45:     }
  46:     private boolean hard(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) >= HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD; }
  47:     private boolean soft(TradeSignal s) { return qualityValid(s) && (decision(s) == SignalDecision.SELL || (decision(s) == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL && confidence(s) < HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD)); }
  48:     private boolean qualityValid(TradeSignal s) { return s != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.getDataQualityValid()); }
  49:     private int confidence(TradeSignal s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.getConfidenceScore(); }
  50:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  51:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
  52: 
  53:     public enum Band { NOT_APPLICABLE, NOT_TRUSTED, SOFT_CONFLICT, HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE, NO_CONFLICT }
  54:     public record Assessment(boolean allowed, String code, Band band, SignalDecision effectiveFiveMinute,
  55:                              SignalDecision effectiveOneHour, Integer maxInitialPercent,
  56:                              boolean hardConfirmationPresent, TradeSignal rawFiveMinute, TradeSignal rawOneHour) {
  57:         static Assessment reject(String code, Band band, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
  58:             return new Assessment(false, code, band, decisionOf(five), decisionOf(one), null, false, five, one);
  59:         }
  60:         static Assessment allow(Band band, SignalDecision five, SignalDecision one, Integer cap, TradeSignal rawFive, TradeSignal rawOne) {
  61:             return new Assessment(true, "HTF_CONTEXT_ALLOWED", band, five, one, cap, false, rawFive, rawOne);
  62:         }
  63:         Assessment withHardConfirmation(boolean value) { return new Assessment(allowed, code, band, effectiveFiveMinute, effectiveOneHour, maxInitialPercent, value, rawFiveMinute, rawOneHour); }
  64:         private static SignalDecision decisionOf(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? SignalDecision.NEUTRAL : s.getDecision(); }
  65:     }
  66: }
