  16: /** FIX-122: evaluation-local history selection. Spring singleton state is thread-bound
  17:  * and restored in finally; open-position additions never enter this scope. Replay uses
  18:  * its own executed stops, never Production history. No wallet lock or balance writes.
  19:  */
  20: @Service
  21: @Slf4j
  22: public class StopLossEvidenceService {
  23:     private final StopLossEvidenceStore store;
  24:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replay;
  25:     private final ObjectMapper mapper;
  26:     private final boolean enabled;
  27:     private final Clock clock;
  28:     private final ThreadLocal<Context> active = new ThreadLocal<>();
  29: 
  30:     @Autowired
  31:     public StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
  32:                                   ObjectMapper mapper, @Value("${trading.fix122.enabled:true}") boolean enabled) {
  33:         this(store,replay,mapper,enabled,Clock.systemUTC());
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
  37:                            ObjectMapper mapper, boolean enabled, Clock clock) {
  38:         this.store=store; this.replay=replay; this.mapper=mapper; this.enabled=enabled; this.clock=clock;
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private boolean enabled() { return replay.active() ? replay.fix122Enabled() : enabled; }
  42:     private Instant now() { return replay.active() ? replay.referenceInstant() : clock.instant(); }
  43:     private StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary resolve(String symbol, Instant at) {
  44:         return replay.active() ? replay.stopBoundary(symbol, at) : store.latest(symbol, at);
  45:     }
  46:     public boolean hasBoundary() { return active.get()!=null && active.get().boundary!=null; }
  47: 
  48:     public ExecutionDecision evaluate(TradeSignal signal, int allocation, Supplier<ExecutionDecision> action) {
  49:         if (allocation>0 || signal==null || signal.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
  50:         return scoped(signal, () -> new SetupWakeupEvaluation(signal, action.get())).decision();
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup(TradeSignal trigger, int allocation, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
  54:         if (allocation>0 || trigger==null || trigger.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
  55:         return scoped(trigger, action);
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     private SetupWakeupEvaluation scoped(TradeSignal trigger, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
  59:         Context previous=active.get();
  60:         Context c=new Context(trigger, now(), replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
  61:         active.set(c);
  62:         try {
  63:             try { c.boundary=resolve(trigger.getSymbol(), c.at); }
  64:             catch (RuntimeException error) {
  65:                 log.warn("[FIX-122] BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE symbol={} signalId={}", trigger.getSymbol(), trigger.getId(), error);
  66:                 c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
  67:                 ExecutionDecision d=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
  68:                         "Stop history could not be checked. Entry deferred to the next normal evaluation.");
  69:                 audit(c,d,"EVALUATION");
  70:                 return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger,d);
  71:             }
  72:             SetupWakeupEvaluation result;
  73:             if ("1m".equals(trigger.getInterval()) && !eligible(trigger,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) {
  74:                 result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger, ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
  75:                         "Current entry candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
  76:             } else {
  77:                 try { result=action.get(); }
  78:                 catch (StaleCurrent ignored) {
  79:                     result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(c.current,ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
  80:                             "Wake-up timing candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
  81:                 }
  82:             }
  83:             if (result!=null && result.decision()!=null) {
  84:                 result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(result.executionSignal(), result.decision()
  85:                         .withStopBoundary(new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Stamp(c.boundary)));
  86:             }
  87:             audit(c,result==null?null:result.decision(),"EVALUATION");
  88:             return result==null?SetupWakeupEvaluation.none():result;
  89:         } finally {
  90:             if(previous==null) active.remove(); else active.set(previous);
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     public void requireCurrent(TradeSignal signal) {
  95:         if(active.get()!=null) active.get().current=signal;
  96:         if(!eligible(signal,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) throw new StaleCurrent();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     public boolean eligible(TradeSignal signal, String purpose) {
 100:         Context c=active.get();
 101:         if(c==null || c.boundary==null) return true;
 102:         String reason=signal==null?"LINEAGE_UNRESOLVED":StopLossEvidencePolicy.exclusion(
 103:                 signal.getInterval(),signal.getCandleOpenTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),c.at,c.boundary);
 104:         if(reason==null) return true;
 105:         Map<String,Object> item=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 106:         item.put("purpose",purpose); item.put("reason",reason);
 107:         if(signal!=null) {
 108:             item.put("signalId",signal.getId()); item.put("interval",signal.getInterval());
 109:             item.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(signal.getCandleOpenTime()));
 110:             item.put("decision",String.valueOf(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if(!c.excluded.contains(item)) c.excluded.add(item);
 113:         return false;
 114:     }
 115: 
 116:     /** Read again at execution-price approval. A newly committed stop invalidates the
 117:      * old approval. This is not a repair of the separate wallet transaction race.
 118:      */
 119:     public ExecutionDecision recheck(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approved) {
 120:         if(approved==null || !approved.allowed() || approved.stopBoundary()==null || !enabled()) return approved;
 121:         Context c=new Context(signal,now(),replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
 122:         ExecutionDecision result=approved;
 123:         try {
 124:             c.boundary=resolve(signal.getSymbol(),c.at);
 125:             if(!Objects.equals(approved.stopBoundary().boundary(),c.boundary)) {
 126:                 c.status="BOUNDARY_CHANGED";
 127:                 result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_CHANGED",
 128:                         "A stop-loss boundary changed after qualification. Wait for a fresh evaluation.", approved.evidence());
 129:             }
 130:         } catch(RuntimeException error) {
 131:             c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
 132:             result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
 133:                     "Stop history unavailable at price revalidation. Entry deferred.",approved.evidence());
 134:             log.warn("[FIX-122] RECHECK_UNAVAILABLE symbol={}",signal.getSymbol(),error);
 135:         }
 136:         audit(c,result,"PRICE_RECHECK");
 137:         return result;
 138:     }
 139: 
 140:     private void audit(Context c,ExecutionDecision d,String stage) {
 141:         Map<String,Object> p=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 142:         p.put("revision","FIX-122"); p.put("source",c.runId==null?"PRODUCTION":"REPLAY");
 143:         p.put("status",c.status!=null?c.status:c.boundary==null?"NOT_APPLICABLE":
 144:                 c.excluded.isEmpty()?"APPLIED_NO_EXCLUSIONS":"APPLIED_EXCLUSIONS");
 145:         p.put("signalId",c.current.getId()); p.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(c.current.getCandleOpenTime()));
 146:         p.put("triggerSignalId",c.trigger.getId());
 147:         p.put("stopExecutionId",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executionId());
 148:         p.put("stopAt",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executedAt().toString());
 149:         p.put("excluded",c.excluded);
 150:         p.put("allowed",d!=null&&d.allowed()); p.put("route",d==null?null:d.source());
 151:         p.put("code",d==null?"NO_WAKEUP":d.code()); p.put("explanation",d==null?null:d.explanation());
 152:         if(d!=null && d.evidence()!=null) {
 153:             p.put("buyCount",d.evidence().buyCount()); p.put("watchCount",d.evidence().watchCount());
 154:             p.put("evidenceScore",d.evidence().evidenceScore()); p.put("health",d.evidence().opportunityHealth());
 155:             p.put("positionPercent",d.positionPercent());
 156:         }
 157:         try { store.append(c.runId,c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.at,stage,mapper.writeValueAsString(p)); }
 158:         catch(Exception error) { log.error("[FIX-122] DIAGNOSTIC_PERSIST_FAILED symbol={} signalId={}",
 159:                 c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),error); }
 160:         if(c.boundary!=null || c.status!=null) log.info(
 161:                 "[FIX-122] {} symbol={} signalId={} stop={} excluded={} code={} allowed={}",stage,
 162:                 c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.boundary,c.excluded.size(),p.get("code"),p.get("allowed"));
 163:     }

 165:     private static final class StaleCurrent extends RuntimeException {}
 166:     private static final class Context {
 167:         final TradeSignal trigger; TradeSignal current; final Instant at; final Long runId;
 168:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary boundary; String status;
 169:         final List<Map<String,Object>> excluded=new ArrayList<>();
 170:         Context(TradeSignal trigger,Instant at,Long runId) { this.trigger=trigger;this.current=trigger;this.at=at;this.runId=runId; }
 171:     }

   8: /**
   9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
  10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
  11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
  12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
  17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
  18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  19: 
  20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
  21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
  24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
  25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  26:         }
  27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
  28:         // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
  29:         boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
  30:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
  31:                         previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  32:                 .isPresent();
  33:         return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  34:     }
  35: }
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.aopalliance.intercept.MethodInterceptor;
   4: import org.springframework.aop.framework.ProxyFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.config.BeanPostProcessor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: import java.util.Set;
   8: 
   9: /** FIX-125: fail before a shared-wallet write/locking read if the caller skipped
  10:  * the global guard. This wraps Spring Data interfaces, including inherited save
  11:  * methods. It is not authorization for arbitrary raw SQL or EntityManager use;
  12:  * the named source-inventory test prohibits adding such wallet writers silently. */
  13: @Component
  14: public class WalletMutationRepositoryGuard implements BeanPostProcessor {
  15:     private static final Set<String> GUARDED=Set.of("walletAssetRepository","walletDailyStatisticsRepository",
  16:         "walletSettingsRepository","walletTradeRepository","walletCashFlowRepository","walletSnapshotRepository");
  17:     public static void check(String repository,String method) {
  18:         if(GUARDED.contains(repository) && (method.startsWith("save") || method.startsWith("delete") || method.equals("flush")
  19:             || method.contains("ForUpdate") || method.equals("creditQuantity") || method.equals("debitQuantityIfSufficient")))
  20:             WalletTransactionCoordination.requireMutation();
  21:     }
  22:     @Override public Object postProcessAfterInitialization(Object bean,String name) {
  23:         if(!GUARDED.contains(name))return bean;
  24:         ProxyFactory proxy=new ProxyFactory(bean);
  25:         proxy.addAdvice((MethodInterceptor) call->{
  26:             check(name,call.getMethod().getName());
  27:             return call.proceed();
  28:         });
  29:         return proxy.getProxy();
  30:     }
  31: }

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 cannot be the governing dual-agent contract as-is. Governance requires the approved exact revision at [`agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:327), but that file remains RPR-0.4 while RPR-0.6 says it supersedes only “for review.” The two documents also materially disagree on scope, modes, and single- versus multi-symbol account behavior.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The claimed “complete” registry is inaccurate. RPR-0.6 names a nonexistent `regression_test_run`; the current run table is `analysis_test_run`. It omits `RegressionTestService`, which creates the run and writes `fix122_replay_revision` before the worker starts ([`RegressionTestService.java:173`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:173)). It also omits required archive/reset changes despite the current exhaustive table purge and ID reset ([`RegressionTestService.java:312`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:312)).

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** M04 is not an implementable staging contract. Current Replay incrementally writes signals, opportunities, executions, positions, timings, and run status; FIX-122 audit writes use an independent `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction ([`RegressionTestWorker.java:636`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:636), [`StopLossEvidenceStore.java:48`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:48)). The artifact does not define staging schemas, promotion atomicity, disposition of independent writes, or how restart/stop/purge paths discard stages.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The proposed read boundary contradicts isolated-account intent. Its permitted evidence includes `signal_processing_work`, which stores `paper_position_id`, and `execution_opportunity`, which records `executed_at` and a Production-derived position percentage ([`B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:907`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:907), [`B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1949`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1949)). `fix122_evaluation` can also contain Production stop execution identity and resulting position percentage ([`StopLossEvidenceService.java:140`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceService.java:140)). The specification must prohibit these as simulation inputs or explicitly limit them to diagnostics.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Strict analysis parity remains under-defined. Production validates data quality before indicator calculation ([`CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java:117`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.java:117)); Replay builds its timeline from every loaded candle without a comparable pre-analysis quality contract ([`RegressionTestWorker.java:550`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:550)). RPR-0.6 does not define the exact historical quality input, finality convention, or dependency-by-dependency as-of rule.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** “Historical recomputation” cannot generically prove parity for sentiment. Historical evaluation reads current mutable provider configuration and deliberately applies different historical/live health logic ([`SentimentService.java:180`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/SentimentService.java:180), [`SentimentService.java:195`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/SentimentService.java:195)). There is no registered versioned historical configuration source in RPR-0.6.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Price ordering is not implementable from the declared artifacts. Current SQL orders accepted events by `(observed_at, id)`, but its `PriceEvent` projection drops both `id` and `ordering_slot` ([`MarketPriceEventService.java:118`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118)). RPR-0.6 requires retained ordering information but does not register the required projection/schema/service changes or the refusal rule for unknown same-boundary visibility.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Isolation is currently only partial, and M05 names no enforceable mechanism. Replay reads `wallet_settings` directly ([`ShadowProductionReplayService.java:79`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:79)); shared validation does likewise ([`TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400)); minimum-notional evaluation can call Binance HTTP ([`BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:38`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:38)). The existing wallet guard covers selected writes/locking reads, not ordinary reads ([`WalletMutationRepositoryGuard.java:15`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletMutationRepositoryGuard.java:15)).

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The artifact conflicts with the stated Replay-only/unchanged-Production-code scope. RPR6-05 and RPR6-07 through RPR6-12 authorize shared Production-class modifications, while omitting central current dependencies such as `ExecutionIntelligenceService`, `ExecutionPriceAuthorityService`, `WalletExecutionSizingPolicy`, and `NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy` ([`ShadowProductionReplayService.java:45`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:45)). Either shared refactoring must be expressly allowed and fully specified, or the design must remain Replay-local.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The proposed analysis oracle is inadequate as an approval control: `ReplayParityValidator` has no production call site and compares only 13 fields, omitting material decision/context fields ([`ReplayParityValidator.java:17`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:17)).

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Resource safety is not actionable. Replay uses unqualified `@Async` ([`RegressionTestWorker.java:99`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:99)); RPR-0.6 gives no named executor, queue/rejection behavior, database budget, or measurable freshness threshold. The configured Hikari maximum is 15 connections.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The reviewed workspace is materially dirty despite its `HEAD` matching the stated baseline commit. Deferring the dirty-tree hash until immediately before implementation permits a different source/config snapshot from the one reviewed. A dual-approval artifact needs its reviewed snapshot fixed before approval.

## OBJECTIONS

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Replace the canonical contract with one hash-identified revision, then have both agents review that exact file and source/config snapshot.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define a complete run state machine: qualification, refusal, private staging, atomic publish, cancellation, crash/restart, archive, purge, and independently committed diagnostics.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Add an evidence matrix that classifies every permitted table/column as diagnostic-only or simulation input, forbidding Production-account-derived inputs.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Specify structural isolation: all account/HTTP dependencies must be virtual ports, with read guards, propagation rules, and negative tests across asynchronous work.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define strict per-dependency no-look-ahead eligibility, including quality, sentiment settings/health, order book, derivatives, HTF/FIX-154 evidence, and price-event visibility.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Register every shared dependency and settle whether shared Production-code modifications are permitted. If allowed, specify method-level equivalence tests and rollback.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Add a named bounded Replay executor and concrete rejection, database, CPU, and Production-freshness thresholds.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No runtime evidence establishes which executor currently services unqualified Replay `@Async`.
- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No database or historical-run evidence was examined, so historical eligibility rates and oracle mismatch rates remain unmeasured.
- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** A correctly revised design may support a useful isolated virtual simulation, but current evidence does not support an `ANALYSIS_PARITY` claim for windows lacking durable contemporaneous context/configuration evidence.
